## Image resource missing alt text
- Code: image_resource_missing_alt
- Severity: low
- Category: Images & media
- Source: resource
- Affected count from run: 1238 assets
- Loaded affected assets in this copy: 71
- Issue summary: Resource evidence stores source page, element, attribute, nearest heading and missing-alt count.

Recommended fix:
- Add missing dimensions and alt text at template level, then verify resource findings after recrawl.
- Add descriptive alt text for content images, or an explicit empty alt attribute only when the image is decorative.
